     CORRECTION:
                  Last month's issue consisted of a grave error of
     a sentence, from paragraph # 8 / last sentence. The article        .
     represented a letter written by L ynn Volpe, vice-president of
     DELVAL-UFO to the ABC network. Through the process of editing,
     the sentence produced an image for DELVAL being of believing
     that UFO and their occupants are Hostile in nature. Lynn wrote
     us after the article appeared last month and informed us that
     DELVAL does NOT believe this to be so. Therefore, we correct
     our mistake and humbly apologize for this wrong interpretation.
     The SBI's philosophy is somewhat in conjunction with DELVAL's
                                                                          ·
     theory. We take an unbias approach that aliens of UFO are not                                                                        )
     either hostile or benevolent, until we find out for ourselves
     for sure. un the other hand, we feel as Jerome Eden does to
     their spacecraft. That being, UFO        crafts    are harmful.to the      (              )
     populace of Eart h due to their polusion emitted gases. ···rn turn,
     we correct the sentence from: ARE YOU AWARE THE U.N. HAS HAD
